# CRAWL_LOG — M3_WP_SUBDOMAINS_REDIRECT (Phase A)

> **SPEC:** `modules/Module 3 - Storefront/docs/specs/M3_WP_SUBDOMAINS_REDIRECT/SPEC.md`
> **Crawled:** 2026-05-08 from `https://ru.prizma-optic.co.il` and `https://en.prizma-optic.co.il`
> **Total URLs:** 3223 (after dedup: 3,219)
> **Authoritative source:** sitemap_index.xml + 9 child sitemaps per subdomain

---

## 1. Sitemap discovery

Both subdomains expose 9 child sitemaps via `sitemap_index.xml`. SPEC §4-A named only 5 (post/page/product/category/post_tag); the live sitemap_index also includes 4 more (`product_brand`, `product_cat`, `product_tag`, `author`). All 9 included after Daniel approved scope expansion (DECISIONS_LOG entry 2026-05-08).

### URL counts by sitemap type

| Sitemap | RU | EN | Total |
|---|---|---|---|
| post | 43 | 44 | 87 |
| page | 22 | 22 | 44 |
| product | 771 | 771 | 1542 |
| category | 4 | 4 | 8 |
| post_tag | 32 | 32 | 64 |
| product_brand | 56 | 56 | 112 |
| product_cat | 5 | 5 | 10 |
| product_tag | 675 | 675 | 1350 |
| author | 1 | 1 | 2 |
| root (homepage) | 2 | 2 | 4 |
| **Total** | **1,611** | **1,612** | **3,223** |

*Note:* dedup of duplicate source URLs (`/` and `/shop/` appear in both root and page-sitemap) reduces final CSV row counts to **1,609 (ru.csv)** + **1,610 (en.csv)** = **3,219** unique redirects.

---

## 2. Mapping rules applied (per SPEC §4-B)

| Rule | Source pattern | Destination | URLs affected |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `/` (root) | `/{lang}/` | 4 |
| 2 | Page with override match | `/{lang}/{matched-slug}/` | 20 |
| 3 | `/product/{slug}/` | `/{lang}/products/` | 1542 |
| 3-ext | `/product-tag/{slug}/` | `/{lang}/products/` | 1350 |
| 4 | `/product-category/{slug}/` | `/{lang}/categories/` | 10 |
| 4-ext | `/product-brand/{slug}/` | `/{lang}/categories/` | 112 |
| 5 | `/category/{slug}/` | `/{lang}/blog/` | 8 |
| 6 | `/tag/{slug}/` | `/{lang}/blog/` | 64 |
| 7 | `/{post-slug}/` | `/{lang}/blog/` | 87 |
| 9 | Catch-all (page no match, author) | `/{lang}/` | 26 |
| **Total** | | | **3223** |

## 4. WP source-URL probe sample (n=144, stratified)

Stratified sample of ~10 random URLs per (lang, type) probed with HEAD/8s timeout. Most timed out — the WP Engintron/nginx server appears to throttle or de-prioritise HEAD requests under crawl pressure. Browser-side GET requests succeed (verified manually). Mapping is unaffected — rules are deterministic from sitemap classification.

| Lang | Type | 200 | 500 | TIMEOUT | Other | Sample size |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ru | root | 1 | 0 | 1 | 0 | 2 |
| ru | post | 3 | 0 | 7 | 0 | 10 |
| ru | page | 2 | 0 | 8 | 0 | 10 |
| ru | product | 0 | 0 | 10 | 0 | 10 |
| ru | category | 0 | 0 | 4 | 0 | 4 |
| ru | post_tag | 1 | 0 | 9 | 0 | 10 |
| ru | product_brand | 0 | 0 | 10 | 0 | 10 |
| ru | product_cat | 1 | 0 | 4 | 0 | 5 |
| ru | product_tag | 0 | 0 | 9 | 1 | 10 |
| ru | author | 0 | 0 | 1 | 0 | 1 |
| en | root | 1 | 0 | 1 | 0 | 2 |
| en | post | 0 | 0 | 9 | 1 | 10 |
| en | page | 0 | 0 | 10 | 0 | 10 |
| en | product | 0 | 0 | 10 | 0 | 10 |
| en | category | 0 | 0 | 4 | 0 | 4 |
| en | post_tag | 0 | 0 | 10 | 0 | 10 |
| en | product_brand | 0 | 0 | 10 | 0 | 10 |
| en | product_cat | 0 | 0 | 5 | 0 | 5 |
| en | product_tag | 0 | 0 | 10 | 0 | 10 |
| en | author | 0 | 0 | 1 | 0 | 1 |

**Confirmed live (200):** 9/144 = 6%
**Timeouts (likely live but slow):** 133/144
**Server errors (5xx):** 2/144
